| R3-P5c-COR-3 | P5c | Major | The run loop's chat call catches only ProviderError plus the new AuditUnavailable, so wrapper refusals (forged_mark, self_check_failed, session_closed, unsupported_input) escape handle as uncaught exceptions and leave the run stuck in WORKING with no test. | OPEN | - | - |

| R1-AKG2-ADV-2 | AK-G2 | Major | The receipt ends done as soon as handle returns even if the run is STOPPED or NEEDS_PERSON, and a retry of handle with the same run_id returns the old half-run, so done does not mean the work was done and the receipt has no link to the final run state. | OPEN | - | - |

| R1-AKG2-ADV-4 | AK-G2 | Major | claim_inbound stamps one lease_until on up to 20 receipts that dispatch runs serially, so a later receipt can have an expired lease before it starts, be re-claimed with attempts bumped without any failure, and reach attempts_exhausted on a healthy but slow queue. | OPEN | - | - |
| R1-AKG2-ADV-5 | AK-G2 | Major | Agent runs execute synchronously in the tick thread with inbound_dispatch last in STEPS, so up to 20 runs can exceed the tick interval and starve sweep, stuck and checkpoint, and the ticket never states how ticks overlap or how long a step may take. | OPEN | - | - |
